## G11 Rejected outright (do not resurrect)

**Manipulative mechanics, refused on principle:** energy · lives · hearts outside a Boss · any cooldown between runs · "next job unlocks in 4 h" · real money · in-app purchase · ads · purchasable hints, streak freezes, cosmetics or continues · loot boxes · hidden drop tables · unrevealed odds · post-hoc reveals · pity timers presented as luck · near-miss animation ("so close!") · a fabricated rival rating on day 1 · notification nagging · badge counts · "your rival is waiting" mail · daily-login reward escalation · appointment mechanics · FOMO windows and "today only" events · streak-loss guilt copy · "you'll lose your rank" warnings · rating or progress decay for absence · a quit penalty, forfeit or "abandoned" flag · a run that cannot be quit · auto-starting the next run · an infinite prestige/rebirth/season ladder · leaderboards against other students · a rival that taunts, speaks or has a face · artificially slow counters and forced animation gates · variable-ratio *rewards* beyond the single disclosed, pre-marked, ×2-capped posting · any mechanic where a die decides a turn · double-or-nothing on XP, mastery, Readiness or a Leitner bucket.

**Mechanics from the eight designs, deleted with their reasons:** CUT, the safehouse tree, intel prices and cases (heist) — currency and a shop, Appendix B · the 99 call (heist) — it was purchasable · Skinned (heist) — replaced by CALL IT · standing coverage / auto-intercept (defense) — removes problems from the session · per-surge Forecast (defense), shift-level Brier (duel), the shot call (season) — duplicate proper scoring rules · the Kelly stake slider (defense, deckbuilder, conquest, season) — a third risk decision per target · the Proctor, the 9-rung rival roster and dossiers (duel), the 7 clubs and the standings (season), the Marker (incremental, deckbuilder), the Fade (conquest) — four adversaries too many · LP, Read, assists, cores, Ground, ATT, doctrine, the Lattice, tactics, tools, relics, modifiers and banners — currency or a second collection · Reforge and plates (incremental) — prestige treadmill · exhibition-decay and daily rating caps as the *only* anti-grind (pushluck, season) — replaced by structural decay (`cold`, `scope`, `tell` retirement, the `w ≥ 0.25` informative gate) that needs no cap · fatigue (season) — a timer on which skills you may practise · the Scout Pack (duel) — a loot box with a pity counter is still a loot box · the 3×3 stance×tactic matrix (duel, deckbuilder) — a second non-transitive game on top of the guard · the Split sealed-bid auction (deckbuilder) — a fourth decision type for one moment a run · Overtime after the Cut (deckbuilder) — "one more" as a default rather than a choice · tag metadata migration on all 68 misconceptions (defense) — derivable from `AREAS` · packet-order or game-chosen item selection — the composer owns what is studied, always · any claim of a 50/50 split that the app does not measure and print.

**Newly rejected in this revision, with their reasons:**
- **A weighted-mean rating** — invariant to its own weights on a homogeneous window, which made farming mastered tier-1 locks the fastest route to the top rank.
- **A pre-call advisor line naming the EV-max rung** — turns a metacognition score into an obedience score. Judges killed it as heist's paid Spotter; free and permanent is strictly worse.
- **An uncapped `cold = 1 + overdue/4`** — the one incentive in the design that pointed away from spaced review.
- **A permanent `tell` keyed on the mere presence of a tag** — buys +25 % on most of the board with 68 deliberate misses.
- **A crew that idles for a whole make for a whole job** — switched forgiveness off exactly where the design prices it.
- **A "growth-optimal band q ∈ [0.52, 0.63]"** — imported from a multiplicative economy; here `E[Δloose]` is strictly increasing in `q` with no interior optimum.
- **A free-and-non-binding COMMIT** — weakly dominant, therefore not a decision.
- **A rank ladder of crime-crew job titles** — costume on a school tool; the ladder now prints the number.
- **A 0.4° envelope tilt** — the one off-grid element in a product built on an 8 px grid.

**Withdrawn at round 4 — claims this document defended and no longer makes.** Every line below was published here, was proved false against the shipped code, and was **deleted rather than repaired**. They are listed because a document may not quietly drop a claim it spent a section defending, and so that round 5 does not re-derive them.

- **“Three states, two flips, no dominant build”**, and Flip 1's cells (STEADY 1.1 / HELD 0.8 on a RUN; HELD 3.5 / 7.4 / 7.3 on the other three). Priced off the drafted queue, STEADY wins every shape by more than 4×; there is no shape flip. The manned cap is the only flip left. (G2)
- **“Flip 2 — mastery state … ordered by `w·(1 − m/100)·encounters`, which is the exact sort key `readiness.weakSpots()` already uses. Same function, two names.”** True, and therefore not a flip: an identity between two expressions of one quantity is not a decision and is not evidence, and a rung with no alternative use is not a state. (G2, G3.8)
- **“You buy HELD for the chain-hold, not the forgiveness”** as a recommendation. The chain-hold is what r2 adds beyond the forgiveness, and it fires once per 84 to 266 jobs. It is not worth a point. (G2)
- **“Mastery strictly dominates forgiveness (the anti-tanking result)”.** The ρ arithmetic survives; the anti-tanking role moves to the rank ratchet. (G2, G3.7 #8)
- **“Tanking is strictly dominated”**, unqualified. It is dominated in the two currencies that gate a tool — rank and carry. It is not dominated in the printed rating against an honest master whose window has emptied. (G3.7 #8)
- **“lowers `q̂` (which moves you off the `w·E[c]` peak)”.** Backwards on the reachable grid: lowering q̂ from 1.0 moves a master **onto** it — and onto it at **q̂ = 6/7, `w·E[c] = 2.4980`**, one throw in seven, not at the 2.2680 in nine-of-ten this list used to name (the reachable grid is `h/of`, not `k/10`). (G3.7 #8; verify r3)
- **“the unfilled slots hold the rating at exactly 5.00” as an anti-farming BRAKE**, and the label **“the farmer's ceiling”**. True as arithmetic, false as a brake: it is the mechanism of the rank demotion the ratchet closes, and 5.00 is what an honest master prints too. (G3.1, G3.7(3)(c))
- **“raisable only by clearing more of the packet”.** The bucket-drop lever exists; it raises the renewable stock rate and lowers the realised take. (G3.7(3)(d))
- **“systematic over-calling drives the rating that gates it to 0” as THE brake.** The rating still falls to 0.00; it no longer takes the rank with it. The brake is the carry ladder, `P(95) = 5.0`. (G3.7 #9)
- **“There is no step in the min-maxer's list that is not also the best available study action, and the proof is arithmetic rather than assertion.”** The arithmetic part is an identity; the claim it was attached to is a per-board measurement (`alignmentFor().claim`, true on 27 % of drafted boards and equivalent to two of the four computed conditions, not implied by all four — verify round 2), and the two argmaxes disagree on 51–93 % of saves depending on the population. (G3.8)
- **Spearman ρ = 1 presented as the MEASUREMENT of that theorem** (G8's J4 row, G9 #5). It is an identity between `crewValue` and `weakSpots()`'s sort key and is now labelled one, with the measured figures printed beside it.
- **“Every number on every screen is recomputable from the save”** without exception. `player.rank` is a stored high-water; the rating that earned it is printed beside it. (G9 #4)
- **“the five keys study progress actually lives in”** as an exhaustive list. `streak` and `jumps` were not in it and were compared by nothing. (G3.7 proof 11, G9 #8)
- **“a job's `finish()` … writes no forecast point”.** A completed job writes one, identically to `#/run/page`. (G3.7 proof 11, G9 #8)
- **“the rating … never removes a tool”** as the rating's own property. It is the rank's. (G5 #4)
- **“Re-press one token with the guard distribution redrawn”** as a live tap that redraws whatever you do, and **“redraw and move nothing”** as an option at all. A free redraw is weakly dominant, therefore not a decision. (G1, §3.4)
- **The printed `x̂` law without its ballast term**, the unconditional “no single job exceeds 25 % of the window”, **the guard fixed point stated without its ε = 0 condition**, and **G8's J3 “< 0.08”** without the window it holds on. (§3.4, G8)
- **“the unexploitable strategy is to mix pressure across skill clusters in proportion to their study value”** attached to `yᵢ = 1 − k/vᵢ`. Three vectors, three owners; that one is the House's mixing, and pressing it is dominated. (§3.4, G3.8 #2)
- **“the debrief says so: `FAC2 crew HELD → STEADY — the Mock says it is not held.`”** Nothing renders that line; `crew.legalize` has no caller. (G4)
- **“posted 186” on the tomorrow line** and **“cost 0.3 rating” on the regret line**. The first printed the whole due queue under the job's own word for its drafted value; the second called a credit gap a rating and overstated it by a factor of 25 to 64. (G5, G6)
- **“The recommended evening shape measures 43 % game … and 51 %”** printed as a measurement of a board this app posts. It is the **nominal** shape's number; the posted boards measure 22 / 30 / 35 % headline. (G1, G9 #1, G10 #17)
- **Six published numerals and captions the shipped functions do not produce as stated:** “2.1 percentage points” (2.04), “−4.90” on the 95 over-calling row (−8.10; −4.90 is the 85 call), “q̂ = the first-attempt rate over the trailing 10 attempts” (the CLEAR rate over the trailing 10 sittings), “the ≥ 80 %-of-peak band … over the four call rungs” (the band is the continuous object's; the discrete one is `[0.779, 0.925]`), “0.900 → 0.143” with neither `S` nor `L` (a 16× spread over realistic states), and “the deep root” as the producer of all eighteen printed `q*` cells (it produces seventeen). (G3.1, G3.2)
- **“composeBundles replicates EVERY critical item into ≥ 3 of the 5”** and **“a walk re-posts the byte-identical board”** without their conditions. The first is bounded by what the shape has room for; the second is true of a walk that answered nothing, and a walk that answered a target is held by `startJob`'s page refusal instead. (G1, G3.7 proof 6)
- **“re-allocation is free and unlimited between jobs”.** There is no between-jobs crew control in `site/js`. (G2)
- **“HELD crew skips the *call* (a pre-stem decision), never the continue.”** No such path exists in any file: `lockCall` is unconditional on crew rank and the call is mandatory on every staked target. G10 #10 is resolved by the bag/push half alone. (G10 #10, G12 #76)
- **`drawnBy: 'job'`** as the field that keeps carry and rating inside the job queue. The field is in no `.js` file, no serialiser and no test; the guarantee is the call graph — `state.applyTarget` is the only writer and `screens/job.js` its only caller. (G3.7 proof 4, G12 #75)
- **“what a throw cannot buy is the rank”** without its condition, and **“tanking is dominated in the two currencies that gate a tool”** without it. Both hold for a student who earned a rung on the way up. A student already competent on the unit never passes through the informative band, holds no rung, and for them a throw buys Called 5 outright and the ratchet keeps it. (G2 “what persists”, G3.7 #8, G12 #74)
- **“water-filling” as the name of the cap projection**, and the `free = { i : i ∉ over }` form of it. The excess is shared in proportion, not levelled, and an index that has been capped stays capped — which is what bounds the loop at ≤ n−1 passes. (§3.4, G12 #10)

**Withdrawn at verify round 2 (the meta lane).**
- **“Desirable difficulty is the only path to rank.”** Fabricated difficulty is the cheaper path: `w` is built from sittings the game does not price, so a review failed on Today's Page buys the same rank for no carry, no chain and no rating. Measured: Called 4 vs Called 2 on 8/8 seeds and +6.91 % post-climb loot, on a byte-identical study ledger. (G3.8 #4, §3.7 proof 8)
- **“Nothing can be farmed.”** The RANK is. The criterion now states its own exception, and G9 says nine of ten pass rather than ten. (G9 #6)
- **“the two agree within 5 points … on both the default and the full-use path”** without a consistent-surface condition. Per surface it holds (worst 1.0 and 1.7 of 20); across a change of surface it does not (worst 6.9 and 7.4, 20/20 and 18/20 over the published 5), on a line still labelled `ledger`. (G9 #1, G8's J8 row)

**Two claims are deliberately NOT published here even though a repair decision specifies them, because the code they describe does not exist in this tree:** r2 as pure two-rung forgiveness with no mastery gate and no chain-hold (the DEEP mechanic), and the parameter-free build inequality over `RUNG_BANDS` that would replace the 4×2 matrix. Both are decided; neither has landed — the deletion is one coordinated six-file change with an open arbiter question under it — and publishing either now would make this document **less** true, not more. G12's round-4 section names what has to land first.
